- **acervo-busca**: Busca e SÍNTESE no acervo local (jurisprudência, teses, doutrina, legislação) e — se a integração estiver ativa — no vault Obsidian, SEMPRE antes da web. Reforça o gate `verificacao-citacoes`: toda afirmação fica ancorada numa fonte verificável do acervo. Use quando o usuário pedir "o que temos no acervo sobre X", reunir teses/precedentes de um tema, ou fundamentar a partir da base interna. Port do padrão "Seeker". - **acervo-conexoes**: Tece o GRAFO do acervo/vault — cria e mantém wikilinks `[[...]]` ligando tese ↔ precedente ↔ caso ↔ pessoa ↔ dispositivo legal. Sugere as conexões ANTES de gravar, com a justificativa de cada uma; faz linking contextual (no corpo, não em listas soltas), bidirecional e seletivo (evita over-linking). Também audita o grafo (notas órfãs, clusters isolados) e propõe "notas-ponte". Use para conectar uma tese aos precedentes que a sustentam e aos casos onde foi aplicada. Requer a integração Obsidian ativa (ou opera com referências no `acervo/`). Port do padrão "Connector". É CONSERVADOR: nunca apaga, só **arquiva/sinaliza**; nunca faz merge automático, **sempre pergunta**. Produz um relatório de saúde. Reusa o `scripts/indexar-acervo.js`. - **adapters**: An adapter is everything atc knows about one agent CLI. The daemon's session core never sees past the adapter interface, so a new agent CLI is a new adapter, not a change to the core. - **adapters**: How Claude Code, Codex, OpenCode, Cursor and any MCP agent are recorded. ([AppMarket-org/app](https://github.com/AppMarket-org/app/blob/HEAD/apps/docs/src/content/docs/agents/adapters.md) · ★1)

- **adaptive-ui-audit-desktop-crosscutting-analyzer**: Desktop + cross-cutting analyzer for the adaptive-ui-audit skill. ALWAYS runs framework-specific adaptive rules, responsive-consistency, and anti-patterns; ALSO runs desktop window-resize (area D1) when the platform includes desktop. Reads the discovery artifact and writes up to four artifacts. Cross findings fold into scored areas by tagging (W*/N*/D1); the anti-pattern step also emits filter verdicts that downgrade/drop weak findings. Adversarial both ways, NO-INVENTED-OBSERVATIONS. ([mcampbellr/engineering-agents](https://github.com/mcampbellr/engineering-agents/blob/HEAD/skills/adaptive-ui-audit/agents/desktop-crosscutting-analyzer.md) · 3 copies)
- **adaptive-ui-audit-native-analyzer**: Native adaptive/responsive UI analyzer. Runs only for apps with platform=native (React Native / Expo AND Flutter). Covers safe-area & insets, keyboard/input visibility, adaptive layout (tablet/orientation/text-scaling), and touch ergonomics. Reads the discovery artifact and writes four area artifacts with severity + confidence classified findings and file:line evidence. Never applies web/DOM rules. Never invents runtime observations; a visual claim is OBSERVED only if a simulator/emulator render confirms it, else "Static analysis — verify on &lt;device/orientation/font setting&gt;" naming the tool. ([mcampbellr/engineering-agents](https://github.com/mcampbellr/engineering-agents/blob/HEAD/skills/adaptive-ui-audit/agents/native-analyzer.md) · 3 copies)
- **adaptive-ui-audit-orchestrator**: Single entry point for an in-session adaptive UI audit. Runs discovery, determines web/native/desktop scope, dispatches only the scope-relevant analysis subagents in dependency-ordered waves, validates artifacts between waves, and hands the manifest to the report-writer. Never reads source files or writes prose. Stops if no web, native, or desktop UI is detected. Never modifies application code. ([mcampbellr/engineering-agents](https://github.com/mcampbellr/engineering-agents/blob/HEAD/skills/adaptive-ui-audit/agents/orchestrator.md) · 3 copies)
- **adaptive-ui-audit-profiler**: Wave 0 profiler for the adaptive UI audit. Discovers the stack, the responsive/styling system, and safe-area/keyboard/orientation handling, and determines web/native/desktop scope (monorepo-aware) BEFORE any analysis, then writes the discovery artifact all downstream steps depend on. If no web, native, or desktop UI is found, records PLATFORMS=none so the orchestrator can stop. ([mcampbellr/engineering-agents](https://github.com/mcampbellr/engineering-agents/blob/HEAD/skills/adaptive-ui-audit/agents/profiler.md) · 3 copies)
- **adaptive-ui-audit-report-writer**: Wave 2 report writer. Synthesizes all step artifacts into the final 10-section adaptive-UI report, merging cross artifacts into areas, applying the anti-pattern filter, root-cause deduplication, scope-conditional adaptive-UI health scoring, and the observed-vs-static classification, then validates and exports MD + JSON + history. Never reads source files; never invents visual/pixel observations; never modifies application code. ([mcampbellr/engineering-agents](https://github.com/mcampbellr/engineering-agents/blob/HEAD/skills/adaptive-ui-audit/agents/report-writer.md) · 3 copies)
- **adaptive-ui-audit-web-analyzer**: Web adaptive-UI analyzer. Runs only for apps tagged web (and desktop apps that are web-tech in a resizable window, which reuse these web checks). Covers layout & overflow, breakpoints & viewport, adaptive components & media, and interaction & reflow across the viewport matrix. Reads the discovery artifact and writes four area artifacts with severity + confidence classified findings and file:line evidence. Audit-only — never modifies code. Never invents visual observations. ([mcampbellr/engineering-agents](https://github.com/mcampbellr/engineering-agents/blob/HEAD/skills/adaptive-ui-audit/agents/web-analyzer.md) · 3 copies)

- **ADR-ANIM-001-part-rig**: All non-AI creature motion is in `core/src/SpriteMotion.cpp` and `core/src/PixelRig.cpp`. Effects are in `core/src/Effects.cpp`. AI key poses are in `core/src/KeyPoses.cpp` and are driven from `app/src/SpriteAnimateDialog.cpp`. ([AceGears/AceStudio](https://github.com/AceGears/AceStudio/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/ADR-ANIM-001-part-rig.md))
- **ADR-ANIM-002-motion-studio**: What can be extended: the part rig is the right base for retargeting. It already keeps bone lengths, layers parts with depth, and renders in a pixel-art-safe way. What is missing: a 3D skeleton type, motion import, a camera model, editable and saved rigs (ADR-ANIM-001 schema 4), keyframed tracks and curves, and a workspace UI. ([AceGears/AceStudio](https://github.com/AceGears/AceStudio/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/ADR-ANIM-002-motion-studio.md))
- **ADR-ANIM-003-motion-pixel-studio**: - **No paid cloud use without the user's approval**, and no public unauthenticated GPU endpoint. Every AI step stays optional, local first, with an explicit cost prompt when it is remote (`docs/agents/CLOUD_GPU_DESIGN.md`). - **Pixel-art safety first.** Nothing may blend colours or add partial alpha to pixel art unless the user asks for it. That is why the in-betweener below is not a neural interp ([AceGears/AceStudio](https://github.com/AceGears/AceStudio/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/ADR-ANIM-003-motion-pixel-studio.md))
- **ADR-ANIM-004-editable-rigs**: The part rig of ADR-ANIM-001 is rebuilt from the sprite every time a clip is made and thrown away afterwards. Users cannot see it, fix a bad cut, move a joint, add a bone for a tail or a held sword, or keep the rig with the project. ADR-ANIM-003 lists saved, editable rigs as the next stage of phase 3. ([AceGears/AceStudio](https://github.com/AceGears/AceStudio/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/ADR-ANIM-004-editable-rigs.md))
- **ADR-ANIM-005-vector-shapes**: The brief asks for vector shapes (a pen with anchors, rectangles, ellipses, auto-close) rasterised pixel-perfectly, dithered gradient fills and stroke styles. AceStudio already has raster Line, Rectangle, Ellipse, Polygon, Curve and Gradient tools (ordered Bayer dithering included), but what they draw cannot be edited afterwards. ([AceGears/AceStudio](https://github.com/AceGears/AceStudio/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/ADR-ANIM-005-vector-shapes.md))
